Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a machine_learning algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 618. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 124/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 618 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the machine_learning structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 124. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the existence and uniqueness fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 215.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a machine_learning algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 857. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 423/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 857 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the machine_learning structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 423. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
